LightSpeed 7.X Service Methods
Module Version  1.0,  Version Date  2/14/07

GOC5 Message Logs

Last Revised:18 September 2006

1  Overview

GOC5 message logs contain the LINUX operating system messages. Use on Host, VDARC and VIG. To access the logs, perform the following actions:

  1. Open a Unix shell on the specific Node.

  2. Type: cd/var/log

  3. Type: more  messages

The /var/log/messages file can get very long and pose a barrier to performance. So whenever the file size exceeds a certain limit (or by number of days expired), the Linux OS saves the current messages file into a file called messages.1, messages.2 etc and writes fresh on the messages file.

NOTICE
Messages posted in the various var logs will change appearance and content from Software release to Software release.
